Stage 2 of 5; reads the brief that o3sanity:gather left and hands off to o3sanity:draft. ([o3world/o3-sanity](https://github.com/o3world/o3-sanity/tree/HEAD/tools/authoring-skill/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Use when the user wants a short cited memo on a topic from the Brain — gathers narrative, metrics, and relations; verifies claims; then proposes the write to the human for approval, emitting to docs/kb/ with a Sources section and a sources.json sidecar. ([onetest-ai/applied_skills](https://github.com/onetest-ai/applied_skills/tree/HEAD/bundles/kb/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Capture the user's input for a new single-page website design — name, audience, purpose, vibe, optional inspiration references. Writes nebula/brief.md. Asks at most 2–3 clarifying questions if input is sparse. Use when starting a new nebula design or when invoked via /nebula:brief. ([paolomoz/nebula](https://github.com/paolomoz/nebula/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Use when the user invokes /brief, or asks for a quick recap/summary of "what's going on", "where we are", "the current scenario", or "what's happening right now" in the conversation or task — wants the gist fast, not a wall of text. ([paulomtts/leave-me-alone](https://github.com/paulomtts/leave-me-alone/tree/HEAD/plugins/leave-me-alone/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Guided client intake interview that produces brief.yaml — household, mood, taste (LOVE/HATE), priorities, numeric budget, constraints, reference URLs. Use when starting a design brief, when the user describes what they want from their home, or when brief.yaml is empty or missing. ([pepuscz/planhaus](https://github.com/pepuscz/planhaus/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Generates design.md (the green figure) as the handoff artifact for Phase 4 (Build). Always use this Skill at the end of Phase 3 (/design). ([pesteph/agentic-workflow](https://github.com/pesteph/agentic-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Kontrakt epizodu bez ticketu: w jednej turze ustala, jak wyglada 'gotowe' (obserwowalny stan), czego nie ruszac i co agent robi sam, a przed czym sie zatrzymuje; po jawnej akceptacji od razu zaczyna prace. Dziala bez MCP i bez sluga. Uzyj na starcie kazdej pracy, ktora nie jest ticketem: konfiguracja, investigation, dokumentacja, drobna zmiana. ([piotrkrych2/monolynx](https://github.com/piotrkrych2/monolynx/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Quick contextual briefing with pre-flight validation. Scans docs, code, git, and runs health checks before starting work. ([r-bart/devtronic-plugin](https://github.com/r-bart/devtronic-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/devtronic/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Start a piece of design work by writing a brief — goal, audience, output, references, and acceptance criteria — and a checklist of deliverables. Use when the designer is ready to commit to a direction. Takes a name or idea, e.g. "/sdd-design:brief pricing page". ([rohaquinlop/spec-driven-designment](https://github.com/rohaquinlop/spec-driven-designment/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Turn a raw request (often a transcribed voice note) into a brief: four lines and three fields, echoed back for correction, then handed to the ticket contract for a Track B ticket. Use when you type /brief or start a ticket from a request. Manual only. NOT for writing the ticket body itself (docs/factory/CONTRACT.md) or breaking a large request into several tickets (Track C). ([SamyakJhaveri/loam](https://github.com/SamyakJhaveri/loam/tree/HEAD/cultivation/marketplace/sam-cc-setup/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Roll up recent activity — what happened, patterns across it, what's stalled. ([Sebastian-Sole/second-brain-demo](https://github.com/Sebastian-Sole/second-brain-demo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Create a one-shot topical orientation brief from the LLM wiki. Reads hot.md, index.md, relevant concept/entity pages, syntheses, and source pages as needed; saves to 2-outputs/brief/brief-YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM-{topic}.md. Use when the user wants a broad lay-of-the-land note rather than a specific research answer. Also use when the user asks for an overview, the lay of the land, "orient me on X", "catch me up on X", "bring me up to speed on X", "state of the wiki on X", "where are we on X", or a topic primer. Not for a specific research question (use query), a side-by-side of 2-4 named pages (use co… ([sheilaschoepp/llm-wiki](https://github.com/sheilaschoepp/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brief))
- **brief**: The daily decision-and-action launcher, chat-native — triggered by the profile ritual phrase; surfaces what needs a move today with urgency, playbook opinion, and actions. ([shmattox/aios](https://github.com/shmattox/aios/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Phase 1.5 — Explain a planned change before it is built. Runs between spec and approve-plan, so a mismatch costs a conversation rather than a change. Five sections, expanding then summarising — what will be built, why this way, the choices you didn't make, what it touches, and a short summary. Never blocks: it prints, offers one skippable prompt, and returns. Calibrated via .spwf/learner.md. Companion to recap and understand, which teach after the fact; this teaches before. Runs on changes, not bug fixes. ([simon-potter/spwf](https://github.com/simon-potter/spwf/tree/HEAD/plugins/spwf/skills/brief))
- **brief**: 제작 브리프(발주서) 표준 포맷 — 브랜드·상품·목표·타깃·핵심 메시지·오퍼·CTA·채널·규격·수량·사용할 이미지/로고·금지 표현·참고·기한 + 메시지 ID·A/B 가설·성과 회수 방법·예산 상한까지 한 장으로 만든다. "소재 발주서 써줘 / 디자이너한테 뭐라고 넘기지 / 브리프 만들어줘 / 배너 제작 요청서 / 외주 맡길 건데 정리해줘 / 이미지팩토리에 발주"일 때. ([sminju98/marketing-copilot](https://github.com/sminju98/marketing-copilot/tree/HEAD/skills/brief))
- **brief**: Assemble context from Alexandria for implementation tasks. Use when starting a feature, fixing a bug, or making changes that touch product concepts in the library.
